

Last updated: August 16th, 2026

↖️ **On desktop, please click on “Show document outline” for quick access to all the different sections. This document is long and not mobile-friendly.**

There are many body mods for the critically acclaimed MMORPG Final Fantasy XIV \- too many, some might say. This is my meager attempt to compile them all in one place for easy access and comparisons. 

If you’re here, then you probably already have a modding program or two installed, or are looking into them and the rabbit hole of modding in FFXIV. Before any of these bodies can be used, you will need to install one of the following programs (It is recommended to only pick one, as the two do not play nice with each other):

[Dalamud, the custom launcher](https://github.com/goatcorp/FFXIVQuickLauncher) \> [Penumbra](https://reniguide.info/): The current modern modding tool. For those wanting to install and use mods. HIGHLY RECOMMENDED for all.  
*OR*  
[FFXIVTexTools](https://www.ffxiv-textools.net/): The original, legacy modding tool. Only recommended for those wanting to make their own mods and modpacks as it can directly affect the game files. No longer recommended for casual mod use.

*Disclaimer: I have not been modding long (Sept ‘22) so some information provided on older bodies may potentially be inaccurate. If I have made a mistake somewhere please DM me on* [Twitter](https://x.com/MooncatKeeper)*,* [Bluesky](https://bsky.app/profile/mooncatkeeper.bsky.social)*, or file a report on XMA so I can fix it\!*

🔞 **WARNING**: All base body mods, save those specified as SFW, have NSFW previews. All written descriptions include typically NSFW language to describe body parts and anatomy. By continuing to read you agree that you are 18 years or older, or the minimum age requirement where you live, and are allowed to read, see, and consume such content. 🔞  
All websites provided **require** an account to view any 18+ content.

 **\! If you are a mod creator and do not see your content listed and want it added, or want details on your mod updated/changed, please DM me on Twitter or Bsky \!**

---

**Important footnote** 

This does not have *every* single mod available for the modding community \- I only list mods that meet the following qualifications:

* Are free  
* Have a publicly available link  
* Are not made or associated with AI-generated assets

I do not provide any Discord invite links, and I do not provide links to paid mods. If you want a Compendium that includes these mods, along with extras such as Au Ra scales, horns, wings and more, I would recommend checking out [**this Body Compendium**](https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/1JwqWGLDtpFqaFBDD8IIJSMmHymGtJhzKdPRF1az8oGQ/edit?usp=sharing) compiled by Fantosme.

## ♦ **DAWNTRAIL DISCLAIMER** ♦

Dawntrail broke many mods, and XMA will be archiving/unpublishing mods that are not properly updated to work with the expansion. This collection was originally compiled *before* Dawntrail’s release and I cannot promise everything here will have a working link. But I *can* promise I will be vigilant with checking as many of these links and mods as I can to ensure a working, up-to-date list. Any mods confirmed to be updated for Dawntrail will be given a diamond (♦) after their titles.

## **Part 0: Basics for Beginners \- Read this first if you’re new to the modding scene\!**

So, first thing’s first.

**Modding the game Final Fantasy XIV violates the ToS.** By downloading modding tools like TexTools and FFXIVQuickLauncher, you understand that you are breaking the game’s ToS. Myself, plug-in creators, and other mod creators, are not responsible for how you play the game with mods and are not responsible for any possible bans related to mod use.

The general consensus of mod usage is “**Don’t ask, don’t tell.**” **Never *openly advertise* mod usage in-game.** Never. Not in /say chat, not with random people in a party, not in your Adventurer Plate/Note, never have them enabled while streaming, and *especially* do not tag the official SquareEnix/Final Fantasy XIV accounts on social media with modded screenshots. There are certain hashtags that the community uses if you’re going to post modded screenshots on websites like Twitter or Bluesky \- a good rule of thumb here is to avoid posting your character’s full name with the screenshots. But overall, so long as you’re quiet about your mod usage, you should be fine.

Next up, let’s go over just a few words and terms you should familiarize yourself with that I will be using in this document and/or will likely be used in mod descriptions. Many of these I had to learn on my own without anyone telling me what they were so I’ll throw them here, just in case.  
—

“Anamnesis”: A third-party tool that allows you to load in just about any piece of gear onto your character, regardless of whether or not you have it. Can be used to temporarily change your character’s appearance, including race and gender swaps. Widely considered the main tool used for custom “gpose” poses as it allows you to change the positions and rotations of the 3D model bones (prior to Brio and Ktisis’ development).  
	*Disclaimer: As of September 27th, 2023, development for Anamnesis has largely been halted. The program may receive minor updates to keep compatibility with game updates, but may not be compatible with other plug-ins and may have other errors. Known to have compatibility errors with the plug-in ‘Glamourer’.*

“Dalamud”: The alias/nickname for XIVQuickLauncher.

“Diffuse map”: This refers to the texture that you see on your character model. This is what needs to be edited for a custom texture such as tattoos or freckles.

“Gposing”: FFXIV has a custom tool in the base game that allows you to take snapshots/screenshots of your character in most any setting and location in the game called the “group posing tool”, or “gpose” for short. You can even use emotes, facial expressions, and combat abilities to get the pose you want. It also comes with many filters and limited lighting options to work with.

“IVCS”: IVCS is the acronym for 'Illusio Vitae Custom Skeleton'. What it does is add extra “bones” to the underlying 3D model that allows extra options of movement to fingers, toes, breasts, genitals, and butt. Because this is an advanced mod, please see the [official guide](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1yo-ynhqH-gMTN7SQv55x2yzOe_V2II9dSAJE8FnEghE/edit?ref=xivmods.guide) for more information.

"Midlander-based": Most of the races share the same body model, which is what this term refers to. It includes Hyur (Midlander), Au Ra, Miqo'te, Elezen, Viera, and female Roegadyn. Male Highlander Hyur have their own separate body model, and male Hrothgar and male Roegadyn more or less share a body model. If you are a male Highlander, you may have to look for a separate port of some mods.

* "HAMEV": This is an abbreviation you might see in the descriptions of some mods to tell what races it is compatible with. There is also "HAMERV" to include Roegadyn.

“Plug-in(s)”: These are the tools you can use through Dalamud. Certain popular plug-ins include:

* *Brio*: A posing tool similar to Ktisis that also covers animations without having to pause the characters in gpose. Semi-compatible with Ktisis.  
* *Customize+*: A scaling tool that lets you adjust the size of just about any aspect of your character.  
* *Glamourer*: A tool akin to Anamnesis that allows you to load in just about any piece of gear in the game or temporarily change your character’s appearance, but in-game. May conflict with Anamnesis.  
* *Ktisis*: The initial posing plug-in successor to Anamnesis that displays the 3D model ‘bones’ in the game to make posing and rotating the character model a little less jarring than switching between windows. Also allows you to change a character’s appearance and/or gear while in gpose. Semi-compatible with Brio and Anamnesis.  
* *Penumbra*: The main tool for using mods through Dalamud.   
* *Simple Heels*: Adjusts where your character’s model stands to avoid clipping with the floor. Used in accordance with shoe or foot mods that lengthen/change the foot model (i.e. ‘Peets’ or ‘Feral Paws’).

"Port": This means that someone took one mod and made it compatible with another body and/or race. For example if a hair mod was originally made for Au Ra only, taking it and making it compatible with another race is called porting. The same applies to taking models from other games and turning them into a FFXIV mod.

“Skeleton”: This refers to what makes the character’s body move. There are several ‘custom’ skeletons implemented throughout the different bodies that add extra ‘bones’ that help modders make more complex animations than what the base game has to offer. Currently, these skeletons include IVCS/IVCS2, Nofflebones (NFLB), Skelomae, and Yet Another Skeleton (YAS). If you’re having issues with gear distorting your legs, breasts or hands, you may need to install one of these. (Note that these are different from mods that add jiggle physics and using both may lead to other model issues.)

“Normal map”: This refers to the texture that adds shadows to a model. Altering this can help give muscles, scars and tattoos a faux 3D look in certain lighting conditions.

“Upscale”: This is often used in reference to textures with an increased resolution. Can also be used to refer to a gear mod that has been altered to the shape of one or more body models, i.e. from Vanilla to Bibo+. Another more accurate term for this is “Refit” if the body type is changing without altering how the model works, i.e ‘refitting’ a model from base TBSE to TBSE-X.

“Vanilla”: This refers to the base game, with no mods. Usually said in reference to models and textures.  
—

## **Part 1: The Base Bodies**

These are the barebones mods that are necessary downloads to get just about any gear mod working.

**•MALE•**

[**The Body SE**](https://heliosphere.app/mod/tbse) ♦ (by Tsar) \- for **Highlander** and all **Midlander-based** **male bodies**. SFW option available in the base mod. IVCS compatible. High support.

[**The hrBody 3**](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/32159) ♦ (By Raykie, Tsar) (+[SFW edition](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/53320)) \- for all **male** **Roegadyn** and **Hrothgar**. 3 base body types: Medium, Chubby, and Beefy. 3 optional body types: Extra Beefy, Lean Beefy, and Soft Medium.  Backwards compatible with older versions and Vanilla. Includes 4 body hair options for Roegadyn, clawed feet/hands for Hrothgar, and a layered fur option for Hrothgar. IVCS2 compatible. High support.

**•LALAFELL•**

[**Otopop**](https://heliosphere.app/mod/otopop) ♦ (by ulli)(SFW) \- Has 5 different upper body type options with 3 SFW smallclothes tops and 3 smallclothes bottoms. Includes optional freckles and skin pigmentation options. Unisex. Not compatible with other Lalafell bodies. Medium-low support.

[**Re\[de\]fined Lalafell**](https://heliosphere.app/mod/g2avv5x6z50nfefc7vq2846r58) ♦ (By minmo., ToFu)(SFW) \- Also called ReLala. Includes a subtle body blush and options for moles and freckles. Unisex. Not compatible with other Lalafell bodies. Medium support.

* [**Re\[de\]fined Lalafell 3.0**](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/145156) ♦ (By minmo., ToFu)(SFW) \- Also called ReLala. Includes hands with fingernails, better defined toes, a subtle body blush, freckles, Minou Beans, Feral Claws, and Minooves. Unisex. Not compatible with other Lalafell bodies. Devkit available (includes Cherie and Melusine body sculpts). Medium support.

[**Shapely SFW**](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/7898) (By kogmods)(SFW) \- Unisex. Unknown support.

[**Shapely HD4 SFW**](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/102019) (By kogmods)(SFW) \- Updated version of the original Shapely body. Unisex. Unknown support.

**•FEMALE•**

[**Tight\&Firm Gen 3**](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/30601) (by TitanFirm) \- All **female bodies** sans Lalafell. Small, medium, large, and XL breast sizes. Wide hips. Meant to be a more Barbie-like, hourglass-shaped pin-up body. Originally the base model and (legacy) textures for most **Gen3**\-based bodies. Medium-low support.

* [SFW body option](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/32922)

[**Eve \- Female Body Gen3**](https://xivmodarchive.com/modid/15799) ♦ (by Nyaughty) \- A **Gen3** **female** body with an hourglass figure. This body uses its own skin textures but comes with a B+ skin texture compatibility option. 3 breast sizes, 3 genital options. Includes 2 non-vanilla Au Ra scales and Scaleless support. Includes its own pubes material and BPF textures. IVCS2, YAS, and NFLB compatible. Medium gear support; low community support.

* *\*The new 3.6 model (Oct. 2025\) adjusts the waist seam and is not compatible with gear mods made for 3.5 and below.*

[**Freyja**](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/139099) ♦ (by Astrild) \- Slightly different body shape than the base TnF with altered topology and weights. Three breast sizes: Small, Medium, and Large. **Gen3** texture base but comes with Bibo+ pube compatibility, puffy vulva, and compatibility with UNF and BBWVR (Gen2) textures. IVCS compatible. Low support.

[**Pythia**](https://heliosphere.app/mod/pythia_3) ♦ (by scherana) \- A **Gen3** **female** body made to better match the vanilla body size. Custom diffuse texture. 5 breast sizes and 3 butt sizes with pube and piercing options. Legacy Tattoo compatible. TnF Breast Slider use recommended. Compatible with **Gen3**, **Bibo+**, and **Gen2**/Vanilla textures. [IVCS compatible](https://heliosphere.app/mod/m2rsqmxh1n5m17edzpn387sbg4). Devkit included. Low support.

* [SFW body option](https://heliosphere.app/mod/sqbwvnwpch1qxaftgr4x3v1gcm) ♦

[**UNF3 \- Unified Feminine Body Installer (Gen 3\)**](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/45473) (by Sel) \- All female bodies sans Lalafell. Compatible with (almost) any body size edit for **Gen2**, **Gen3**, and **Bibo+** bodies that only affects the model for the ultimate mix-and-match possibilities. The base mod includes 7 chest models, 12 leg/genital options, 7 pube options, 15 total piercing options, and 2 muscle tones. Supports Scaleless Au Ra. Devkit included. No known support.

---

[**Bibo+**](https://heliosphere.app/mod/bibo) ♦ (by bizu, Tsar, Illy Does Things) \- All **female bodies** sans Lalafell. The base model includes 3 breast sizes and 3 hip sizes with multiple texture options. IVCS compatible. Includes **Gen3** texture compatibility. High support. SFW options included in the base installation.

[**Muse+**](https://www.glamourdresser.com/mods/muse) ♦ (by Kydeimos) \- An androgynous-shaped **Bibo+** based body sculpt, this body is intended for “trans, enby or otherwise gender non-conforming characters” that use the female model in-game. This body features a flat chest, slightly reshaped leg models, reshaped butt, and reworked weights. 5 vulva types (A, B, C, plus 2 bottom growth options), 3 penis size options, each with a flaccid/erect option and a cut/uncut option. Includes a custom ‘Soft’ belly shape inspired by Rue+ (which breaks the vanilla waist seam). Includes SFW option in the base download. IVCS compatible. Low support. [YAS compatible](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/113643).

[**Neolithe**](https://heliosphere.app/mod/wsnqd8bt5h0bd2rezscmdg1zew) ♦ (by Major Praline, Lye) \- The upgraded version of Lithe+ with its own topology, weights, and a(n optional) custom skin texture. Features include pronounced hip dips, thin shins, hourglass figure, slim hands & feet, custom trans addon, custom piercings, and “Neobelly” (called ‘Libelly’ during testing) \- a slightly larger midsection. 4 breast sizes with 4 shapes, 3 hip sizes, 2 shin sizes, 4 labia types, and dyeable finger/toe nails. SFW option available in base install. Includes ‘BPF’ support. Compatible with **Bibo+** based textures. Devkit available. Brand-new (Public release May 2025); Medium-low support. *\*Neobelly breaks the vanilla waist seam \- Not cross-compatible with other body edits.*

[**Tre**](https://heliosphere.app/mod/ftaxs2xnvh7d75v2yvwf0tmsb4) ♦ (by SB\!/Skateboards) \- A bottom-heavy body sculpt with custom hands and feet with custom topology & weights for the butt, hips, and chest. 3 breast sizes, 2 butt sizes. IVCS/YAS compatible. Devkit included. Utilizes **Bibo+** based textures. Includes Bibo+ Trans support with a custom model. Medium-high support. SFW options included in the base installation.

[**Yet Another Body**](https://heliosphere.app/mod/yab) ♦ (YAB) (by Aleks) \- A high-poly body model that alters the upper body, arms, and legs to have a larger, buff model with its own topology and weights. 3 breast sizes, 2 leg sizes, 2 butt sizes. Buff musculature optional. Includes custom nipple piercings, slim feet, dyeable toenails and dyeable fingernails. Scaleless Au Ra support. Customize+ use encouraged. Includes ‘BPF’ support. Devkit included. Utilizes **Bibo+** based textures. Includes ‘Yiggle’ body physics. High support.

* [SFW option](https://xivmodarchive.com/modid/109134) (pre-DT)

*Well that doesn’t seem too bad*, you think to yourself. 

So… Here is where it starts to get complicated. Because all of these non-Lalafell bodies are highly customizable, there are many custom edits of Bibo+, TBSE, TnF3, and even a few for hrBody. I’m going to call these *edits*.

The Body, hrBody, Bibo, and TnF are largely considered the “original” bodies, and even the standalone bodies use one of their texture maps/UVs as a base. Keep in mind that these UVs are what divide the Bibo+ and Gen3 bodies and they are not always cross-compatible with another body. (For more information on UVs and the texture differences between Bibo+ and Gen3, please refer to [this guide](https://reniguide.carrd.co/#skininthegame).) For a visual reference to see the differences between some of the Midlander-based female bodies, please see [this comparison chart](https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/1qQzuE17E7c0aMqfpS1OIePKPGhMwnzipmyy2hu6hrBY/edit?usp=sharing) compiled by [Yangi](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/user/470557), or [this comparison chart](https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/1RbWGuqlcab5jFVF4-lmhpoMlgO77zpGKBfV51YrjG_U/edit?gid=0#gid=0) compiled by [Diacred](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/user/96473).

Nearly every body mod has at least one derivative, because people enjoy making custom bodies that fit their own character to match how the person views them. Not every single one of these body edits is widely used or supported, but the sheer number of how many there are is why I decided to make this index in the first place.

These will be listed in alphabetical order and with their own edits and add-ons. Lalafell bodies and add-ons will be listed together as many mods made for them are also compatible with the base-game vanilla Lalafell body.

## **Part 2: Bibo+**

Bibo+ is one of the most commonly used bodies for Tall Females with just about any body shape you can imagine available. Most every body edit here is compatible with Customize+ to get your character *just right*. Most of these body edits will require the base Bibo+ body installed to work as they only include the model and not the necessary textures. And, unless specified otherwise, can be combined in a mix-and-match style.

### **2.1: Bibo+ Body Edits**

	[Andro+](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/115592) ♦ 

* Flattens the breasts and widens the torso and chest for an androgynous shape. Includes optional flat breast textures and optional nipples.

	[AnimeBody+](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/117348) ♦

* An unrealistic, exaggerated hourglass shape with a small waist, hips wider than the shoulders and thick thighs. 1 breast size, 3 vulva options. BPF compatible. No known support. 

	[Aphrodite+](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/71890) (DISCONTINUED)

* A thick and curvy sculpt that gives the body wide hips, large breasts, bigger thighs and a thicker waist. Comes with soft belly or muscular belly options. Customize+ body scaling highly recommended. Unknown support.

### **2.2: Bibo+ Breast Sizes**

These body edits only affect the breast size (chest model) of the Bibo+ body and require the base mod in order to work. Compatible with most leg models unless they break a vanilla seam \- i.e. a Rue+ or Citrus+ belly.

For a visual comparison sheet of some base model default chest sizes, please see Singasong/Diacred’s guide [here](https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/1RbWGuqlcab5jFVF4-lmhpoMlgO77zpGKBfV51YrjG_U/edit?gid=908427890#gid=908427890).

* [Adagio Chest for YAB-based Bodies](https://payhip.com/b/rxmGb) ♦ \- A flat chest inspired by Muse and TBSE Echo. Rue belly compatible.

* [~~Aphrodite+ Small Chest~~](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/92816) ~~\- Gives the Aphrodite+ body small breasts. Unknown support.~~

* [B+ Masc Chest](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/100963) ♦ \- A mostly flat chest with no breast weights/physics and small amounts of muscle definition.

* [Bacchante](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/33681) \- One of the largest Bibo+ sizes. Hefty and sits low on the torso with natural sag and forward-facing nipples. Low support.  
  * [Bacchante \+ Poseable/Weighted/Scale-able/Physics](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/69360) \- Gives the Bacchante breasts different weights to make scaling and posing easier. Intended for nude use.

* [Bibo+ XXL](https://www.glamourdresser.com/mods/bibo-xxl) \- Four chest sizes, bigger than the base Bibo+ options but smaller than Uranus. Close to the RiderTHiCC-M model. Unknown support.

* [~~Bibo+ Plush Bacchante~~](https://beta.aetherlink.app/mods/7020143111947190272) ~~\- Ports the Bacchante chest size to Bibo+ Plush. Unknown support.~~

## **Part 3: Gen2**

This older female-based body model uses symmetrical textures as opposed to the asymmetrical textures used by Gen3 and Bibo+. Gen2 bodies are largely considered obsolete and outdated by the introduction of Gen3, but they can technically still be used, though nobody still makes mods for them. Download these at your own discretion\!

## **Part 4: Gen3**

Gen3 bodies are generally more popular with the older modding community, and while they may look similar to Bibo+ they use an entirely different texture map/UV. Most DT-updated bodies are compatible with Bibo+ textures and can even be mixed-and-matched with Bibo+ bodies that do not break the vanilla waist seam. 

### **4.1: Gen3 Body Edits**

[AB Body](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/101701) ♦

* A petite body made using the Eve body and textures as a base with Freyja hands, legs & feet. Made for a thinner, small chested look. Includes 3 nipple color and 2 vulva color options. Supports Scaleless Au Ra. Includes Bibo+ pube compatibility. Low support.  
  * [AB Body\_Alter \[Addon\]\[Gen3\]](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/147152) ♦ \- Comes with new breast sizes, new nipple and tummy mesh, and 10 labia types. BPF compatible. Brand new (Sept. 2025); unknown support.  
  * [AB Grippers](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/120942) ♦ \- Hi-poly foot model based off of ‘Le Pied’. Includes heel guards, toe rings, dyeable toenails, and optional dirty sole textures. IVCS/NFLB compatible. Devkit included.  
  * [AP Body](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/123106) ♦ \- Femboy/trans/androgynous body using AB as a base. Includes a flat chest, 5 penis size options (in cut and uncut), and 3 transmasc/t-dick size options. IVCS/NFLB compatible. Unknown support.  
  * [FreyjaAF](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/165455) ♦ \- The successor to the retired Freyja body with matching shapes of the original using the AB Body mesh. 5 breast sizes, 2 leg options, multiple skin texture options. Includes compatibility with Bibo+, Vanilla, Gen3 and Eve textures.

* A plus-size body featuring wide hips, thick thighs, and side chub. Has 3 breast sizes and 3 waist sizes. Includes pubic hair and armpit hair toggles. No known support. *\*Breaks the vanilla waist seam \- Not cross-compatible with other body edits.*

Includes custom textures, normal maps, diffuse maps, skin material, and single wooden leg/hand texture. Supports Scaleless Au Ra. Gen3 texture compatible only. Devkit available. SFW option available. Low support.

Uses AB body chest and Freyja legs as its base. 4 breast sizes, 5 nipple types, 5 genital options. 3 skin types, each with a freckled option (6 total). Supports Scaleless Au Ra. Gen3 texture compatible only and requires any Gen3 body installed to work. Devkit available. SFW option available. Relatively new (Late March 2025\) \- unknown support.

The texture is a mix between Bibo+ and Gen3 but requires a Gen3 base body to work. Comes with an erect nipples option and a womb tattoo. IVCS2 compatible. Very low support.

The hrBody is for *male* Roegadyn and Hrothgar, as they share the same body model. But not all mods affect both races.

## **Part 9: Mixed Bodies and Race-Specific Edits**

Sometimes mods are made to be compatible with multiple body types, are not attached to any body type and are therefore compatible with all of them, or are made with specific races in mind. I’m putting those here\!

*Side note: For the most part, every mod listed here either swaps vanilla assets or modifies them to some degree. As of May 2025, I have started attempting to compile custom ear and tail replacements but note that this will forever be a work in progress. If you cannot find what you’re looking for here, you are welcome to browse the assortment of modding websites at your leisure.*

Looking for more Ear/Tail replacements? Please see [this spreadsheet](https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/1bD--rqyeIPwuavEbViOci7u60J6twO6dIyVPGlkcL60/edit?usp=sharing), or another resource.

Looking for Au Ra Scales? Please see the [Au Ra Collection](https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/1JCHCPPHjtovaIyiHUnpOkCrkIlxBnxclmWX0K2SzN0A/edit?ref=xivmods.guide#gid=1492959006), [Au Ra Compendium](https://chrismods.notion.site/The-Au-Ra-Compendium-1c4e3eb8392680dcabe6d77c29dc43e6), or another resource.

Looking for face sculpts? Please check [Male Face Sculpt](https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/1yzLWRxSER2hQLwF2hhYHrQL1iEE0-fPHMQOsk6x0Ps4/edit?gid=0#gid=0), [Female Face Sculpt](https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/u/0/d/12bQPyWqv8SVWreM5h_-ilC1vFHcsYhj5tWucZv0E7Zw/htmlview), [Free Sculpts Spreadsheet (Pre-DT)](https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/1hSRFspUXRJ1NXUimZTVWu5Zyv-jzV1ntgameqYPxACs/edit?gid=891316374#gid=891316374) / [(DT Updated)](https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/1g_TfNeWYl0RU8O7u-g_dzjXy2edO_zUjeBGeahBXq3U/edit?usp=sharing), or another resource.

  * [\[BPF\] Pack 3: Curly](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/145299) ♦ \- Dense curly hair. 6 patterns.

### **9.2: Race-Based Add-ons**

#### **9.2.1: Au Ra Based** 

* [7.4 Spoiler NPC Face & Skin](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/152807) ♦ \- Scale textures, head model, and horn and tail models from an NPC introduced in the 7.4 DT patch.

* [AuRa Full Body Dragon Scales](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/87462) \- Completely overhauls the female Au Ra body texture and face 4 texture to give both Raen and Xaela a more full-body-scaled, creepy, draconic appearance. Bibo+ body base.

  * [AuRa Dragon Face 2 Xaela and Raen](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/87797)  
  * [AuRa Dragon Face 3 Xaela and Raen](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/87909)

* [Better & Clean Eyebrows for M. AuRa Face 1-101](https://heliosphere.app/mod/mcpepdmtb11eb1q3acxg02bfam) ♦ \- Custom eyebrows for vanilla faces. Not compatible with custom sculpts.

* [Black Sclera 04-104\!](https://neverminding.gumroad.com/l/zzsWdv?layout=profile) \- Black sclera texture edit for Xaela face (4/104).

* [Black Sclera for AsymFaces Raen Female](https://aetherlink.app/mods/7190922296398479360) \- Compatible with all 4 faces for Raen only.

* [Blue Tongues for Female Raen](https://www.nexusmods.com/finalfantasy14/mods/83) \- Changes the entire mouth interior of female Raen characters to a blue hue. Will apply to player characters and NPCs. Not compatible with face upscale mods.

* [Cheek Scales Fix for Au Ra Face 4](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/110083) ♦ \- Fixes a vanilla texture error for female Raen Au Ra face (4). 

* [Dark Brows for All Female Au Ra Faces](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/65727) ♦ \- Gives female Au Ra dark, undyeable brows that will not change to match hair color. Should work with sculpts.

* [Dark Brows for Male Aura](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/65939) ♦ \- Gives male Au Ra dark, undyeable brows that will not change to match hair color. Should work with sculpts.

* [Darker Female Xaela Scales](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/75682) ♦ \- Changes the color of female Xaela scales to a darker shade to match the default male color.

* [Double chin faces for F Au Ra](https://heliosphere.app/mod/fyf1emxt6x09574eym22dyzb7c) ♦ \- Face sculpt edit of the vanilla heads to have a double chin. Affects all faces. Includes ‘Horn Roulette’ compatible option. Includes optional ‘normal’ map to soften the jaw shading.

* [Endwalker aura face 1&101 sculpt adapt to dawntrail](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/121836) ♦ \- The original female Endwalker Au Ra face (1/101) ported to Dawntrail. Includes original EW scale pattern+color. 

* [Endwalker aura face 4&104 sculpt adapt to dawntrail](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/122405) ♦ \- The original female Endwalker Au Ra face (4/104) ported to Dawntrail. Includes original EW scale pattern+color.

* [Fem Au Ra F2 x Everyone Bites \- Multiple Toggles](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/149784) ♦ \- 4 different horn options, 10+ teeth options for female Au Ra face (2/102), and a split tongue option. Includes no horns, Vanilla horns, ‘Fishie’ horns, and ‘Edgy Fish’ horns.

* [Female Au Ra Raen Blue Tongue](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/112576) ♦ \- Texture edit to make female Raen tongues a bold blue. All faces. Should be custom sculpt compatible.

* [Fish Ra Forehead Remover](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/120140) ♦ \- Removes the forehead scales from female Au Ra faces (2/102).

* [forehead scales remover (face 2 femra)](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/145242) ♦ \- Removes the forehead scales from female Au Ra faces (2/102).

* [Izumiko's Au Ra Face 3 Remaster](https://heliosphere.app/mod/et6ambrb8n1f92392c15cptyrc) ♦ \- Alters female Au Ra face (3/103) to resemble the original pre-DT update. Includes ‘Chocochomps’ teeth. Blender kit available.

* ["Legacy" Female Xaela Scale Color](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/122281) ♦ \- Changes the female Xaela scale color to more closely match the original, pre-DT scale color. Vanilla textures only.

* [Male Au Ra Blue Tongue Restore](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/112571) ♦ \- Texture edit to make male Raen tongues a bolder blue. All faces. Should be custom sculpt compatible.

* [Male Au Ra Face 4 Brow Overhaul](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/148146) ♦ \- Better eyebrow textures for male Au Ra face (4/104). Should be compatible with custom horns, face/makeup textures, sculpts and custom horns.

* [mamba (fem au ra mouth recolour)](https://heliosphere.app/mod/7s0s3n4cn91j748zckpzg2yyem) ♦ \- Turns the gums and tongue black.

* [Neck Connector Fix](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/100641) ♦ \- Connects the seam between the body and the neck if you have a gap between them. Also works with black neck tattoos. May not completely erase the neck seam. Compatible with **Vanilla**, **Bibo+**, and **TBSE** bodies.

* [Remove Limbal Ring Glow for Au Ra](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/125002) ♦ \- Removes the glow effect and better matches the ring color to the chosen color swatch from Au Ra limbal rings for Raen and Xaela. Unisex.

* [Sunkissed Freckles for Au Ra](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/160638) ♦ \- Clustered freckles across the cheeks and nose for all Au Ra faces. Unisex. Technically works for all races but made for Au Ra.

* [Trickster](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/135568) ♦ \- A serpent-like horns and tail set with optional decor and snakes. 2 colors, 4 shine options. Decor comes with 2 metal colors and 6 snake texture options.

* [Xaela Face 2 \- Forehead Scales Removed](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/112129) ♦ \- Removes the two forehead scales on male Xaela face (2).

##### **9.2.6.1: Miqo’te Ears**

	Non-feline ears can be found in the ‘Hybrid Resources’ tab.

* [Chili](https://trello.com/c/lw7Gkq9R/98-chili) \- Long and pointed, facing upwards and outwards. Does not auto-hide ear flaps. Female only.

* [Curled Ears](https://heliosphere.app/mod/2zmp1v8wps1dq7rz5mc2rkxqw4) ♦ \- Miqo’te ears curled back. On an accessory and does not fix holes in vanilla hairs. Technically “fit” to all midlander-based tall races but YMMV.

* [Da Poo Ears](https://www.deviantart.com/bizuart/art/Da-Poo-Ears-904062705) \- Long and pointed with inner ear fluff and facing slightly outward. Assumed female only.

* [Ear Flaps Be Gone](https://ko-fi.com/s/f1cea9ab1c) \- Changes the ‘normal’ textures to remove the inner ear flaps for all **male** Miqo’te faces.

* [Ears](https://www.deviantart.com/bizuart/art/Ears-905067634) \- Triangular with a rounded tip, a wide base and inner ear fluff. Assumed female only.

* [earless fem miqo (7.0, 7.1, and 7.2 models)](https://heliosphere.app/mod/rzywb0th292q51vz560ta30ebr) ♦ | [masc](https://heliosphere.app/mod/fnxtxxxv3x3fb29v5t3m6r9hwc) ♦ \- Removes the inner ear flap of Miqo’te ears. Compatible with 7.0, 7.1 and 7.2 head models.

* [Earless Miqo'te Faces](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/71125) ♦ \- Removes the inner ear flap from vanilla Miqo’te heads. Intended for use with earless Miqo’te hair and custom ears.

* [F Miqo Vanilla Ear-Flap Remover「DT」](https://heliosphere.app/mod/0wbwkamg8h3h1be0phtyhnpaz8) ♦ \- Removes the inner skin ear flaps from all female Miqo’te faces.

* [fat fuck ears ✰ for miqo'te](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/160947) ♦ \- Small, triangle-shaped ears sitting on top of the head. Does not automatically hide inner ear flaps. Unisex.

* [Hidden Earflaps for AsymFaces (MiqoF)](https://www.nexusmods.com/finalfantasy14/mods/1995) ♦ \- Removes earflaps (the inner skin) from female Miqo’te faces. Only for use with ‘AsymFaces’. Intended for use with earless hairstyles.

* [Howling Ears for Miqo'te](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/133428) ♦ \- Rips the ears from the Arcadion Boss 5 model and gives them to Miqo’te. 3 options \- Vanilla ear placement, upright side head placement, and droopy side head placement. Requires earless hair. Does not cover ear holes.

* [Hrothgar Ears for Miqo\!](https://heliosphere.app/mod/4js9k2pfeh68d3saqff77697f8) ♦ \- Does not auto-hide ears. Replaces tail (2). Unisex.

* [Male Miqo'te Faces Ear Flap-less](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/37466) \- Removes the inner ear flap from all male Miqo’te heads. Includes toggles to pick and choose which to apply to.

* [Meremono Ears\!](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/157615) ♦ \- Upright triangle-shaped ears on top of the head with inner ear fluff. Includes optional ear jewelry. Female only.  
  * [Meremono Ears v2\! Flat \+ Tiara](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/157929) ♦ \- Reshapes the ears to be horizontal and on the side of the head. Includes optional floating tiara accessory and breath VFX.

* [\[7.1+\] Mikitten Too](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/84707) ♦ \- Replaces Miqo’te ears with the child Miqo’te ear model. *Note: This is an edit of the original mod that has since been deleted and I am currently unable to find a link for.*  
  * [\[7.2+\] Mikitten All Ears](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/98233) ♦ \- 5 more color options. Not compatible with MiKitten Too. Intended for use with vanilla tails and modded tails that do not have ears included.

* [Milkyboo's Tester Ears](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/166018) ♦ \- Overhaul of a taken down ear mod. Large and triangular. Includes multiple position options.

* [Mio's Ears (from Xenoblade 3\) ALL FEMALE MIQOTES](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/136363) ♦ \- Small cat ears. 2 options available for vanilla tail or no tail.

* [Miqo'te Ear Remover \+ Restorer](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/124039) ♦ \- Made to be compatible with **7.2** head model changes. Has 3 options: Remove inner ear flap, restore outer ear fur, and an option to swap male Miqo’te ear models. **Unisex**.

* [Miqo’te Ear Restorer](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/60904) \- Meant for use with hair mods that only come in earless models. Equips via Emperor’s New Earrings.

* [~~PeePoo Ears~~](https://ko-fi.com/s/8923978ac0) ~~\- Tall ears with inner fluff. Female only.~~  
  * [PeePoo Ears for Male Miqo](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/73544) \- Not highlight compatible. Does not auto-hide inner ear flaps.

* [Remove Miqote VANILLA Ear Flaps](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/110053) ♦ \- Removes ear flaps from all male and female Miqo’te faces. Vanilla only.

* [Round-ish Ears](https://aetherlink.app/mods/7042284459542216704) \- Small, rounded ears with inner ear fluff.

* [Shagcoat ears for male miqo (tail install)](https://heliosphere.app/mod/4wcghac1cd2nk0mrk1eqqt8xkc) ♦ \- Ears from the Shagcoat cat mount for male Miqo’te. 

* [Subsurface Scattering For Miqote](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/146533) ♦ \- Affects the ‘mask’ texture so the inner ear flap can now have a minor glow when hit with light.

* [Traditional Anime Cat Ears](https://heliosphere.app/mod/q20z64h3xx7s956qs289ecnvd8) ♦ \- Triangle-shaped, upright ears for female Miqo’te. Affects all tails. Unisex.  
  * [Traditional Anime Cat Ears Ver.2](https://heliosphere.app/mod/z3eatnjfk115v8xe5vq5mjcsxc) ♦ \- A slightly different triangle shape with the addition of inner ear fluff. Affects all tails. Unisex.

##### **9.2.6.2: Miqo’te Tails**

	Non-feline tails can be found in the ‘Hybrid Resources’ tab.

* [› rawr (tail)](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/145842) ♦ \- Short and thin with a fluffy end. Affects tail (1). Female only.

* [Alma's Lion Tail for Miqo'te \[All Genders\]](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/40152) ♦ \- Alters tail (2) to have longer fur at the tip. 2 hair color options. Unisex.

* [Bushy Tail for ALL Miqo'te](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/36712) \- Big fluffy tail. Replaces tail (3). Unisex.  
  * [Tipped Bushy Tail for Miqo'te](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/55592) \- Adds a highlighted tip.

* [Delta's Tail (Female Miqo'te Tail 2\)](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/163031) ♦ \- Stylized furry animal tail. Female only. Replaces tail (2).

* [Female Miqo'te Vanilla Fluffier Tail](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/124391) ♦ \- Vanilla female tail (3), but fluffier.

* [Floofier Tail For Miqo'te Female](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/3359) \- Absolutely massive and fluffy. Replaces all tails. Not highlight compatible. Female only.

* [Fluffy Tail](https://www.deviantart.com/bizuart/art/Fluffy-Tail-904078000) \- Large, long, and rounded. Assumed female only.

* [Fluffy Tip Highlights\!](https://neverminding.gumroad.com/l/RPWsO?layout=profile) \- Changes the tail 3 model to have a tip that dyes with highlights.

* [Fox Tail for Miqo'te Female Tail 3](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/37258) \- Thick yet sleek. Female only.

* [Furrine Tail](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/82200) ♦ \- Long and furry. 3 shapes. 12 patterns. Includes optional mashup with ‘PeePoo’ ears.

* [High Poly Vanilla Miqo’te Tails \+ Texture Upscales](https://www.glamourdresser.com/mods/high-poly-vanilla-miqote-tails-texture-upscales) \- Vanilla female tails, but less blocky. Includes upscaled textures. Works with all tail models.

* [Higher Miqo'te Tails](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/115217) ♦ \- Raises the placement of Miqo’te tails to be higher on the back. Unisex. Sized to fit Annabelle+ and TBSE Orion butts but should be compatible with any body.

* [Higher Poly Miqo'te Tails](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/157355) ♦ \- Vanilla Miqo’te tails, but less blocky. Unisex.

* [I Heard You Like Fluff](https://aetherlink.app/mods/7014954988502024192) \- Long and fluffy with an option for twin tails\! 6 highlight pattern options. Replaces tail (3). Female only.  
  * [rha's I Heard You Like Fluff for M Miqo'te](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/70123) \- Male port. No highlights. Replaces tail (3).

* [Improved Miqo'te Tail 2](https://www.tumblr.com/eastlaketea/762184354288025600/improved-miqote-tail-2-dt-updated) ♦ \- Hi-poly adjustment, plus gold deco. Unisex.

* [Lion Tip Highlights\!](https://neverminding.gumroad.com/l/mOfSq?layout=profile) \- Changes Miqo'te tail (2) to have a colored tip to match the hair highlights.

* [Longhair Tail\!](https://neverminding.gumroad.com/l/amNaX?layout=profile) \- Massive, long, with long-haired fluff. No highlights. Replaces tail (3). Unisex.

* [Lupin tails for female Miqo'te](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/16424) | [Male Miqo’te](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/16214) \- Yanks the tail model from the Lupin NPCs. Matches highlight color.

* [Luxa](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/70306) \- Thick, fuzzy, and long. Replaces tail (1). Female only.

* [\[Miqo'te\] Tiny little tail](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/72389) \- Replaces Miqo’te tail (1) with a small, fluffy, upward-pointing tail. Female bodies only.

* [Miqo'te Twin Tails](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/4786) \- Vanilla model edit. Replaces all tails. Unisex.

* [moonward's bobcat tail](https://heliosphere.app/mod/m8xyasjz857rnfgde3f6v3mrd4) ♦ \- A shorter, rounded tail for Miqo’te tail (2) made from Hrothgar tail (2). Unisex.

* [Panthera (Hrothgar Tail Port)](https://www.glamourdresser.com/mods/panthera-hrothgar-tail-port) ♦ \- Replaces Miqo’te tail 8 with a Hrothgar tail. Has all 4 models with 12 pattern options, plus highlight options. Works for males and females.

* [PUBBY\!](https://heliosphere.app/mod/fjqpgt5sz50zz3xrz8qmmbrkp0) ♦ \- Replaces tail (3) with the tail from the Torgal mount. Unisex.

* [Puff Floff](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/73448) \- Upright pointed ears and a big fluffy tail. Replaces tail (3). Female only.

* [Shagcoat cat tail for Miqo'te](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/143851) ♦ \- The big fluffy tail from the Shagcoat cat mount. Replaces tail (3). Unisex.

* [Super Fluff](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/7911) ♦ \- A thick and fluffy tail. 2 lengths. Replaces tails (1, 2, 3, 4). Female only.  
  * [Gentle Tail](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/55297) ♦ \- Thinner version of the original ‘Super Fluff’ tail. Replaces all 8 tails. Female only.

  * [Super Fluff Highlight Glow (tail 3 Miqo)](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/86538) \- Makes the tail tip color glow. Affects tail (3). Female only.  
    * [Super Fluff Highlights Glow (All Miqo)](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/86545) \- Makes the tail tip color glow. Affects all tails. Unisex.

* [Twin Tails Fem Miqo](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/113091) ♦ \- Vanilla model edit. Replaces all tails. Female only.

* [Vanilla Hrothgal tails for F\! Miqo](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/116143) ♦ \- Replaces female Miqo’te tails with the female Hrothgar tails. Affects all tails.

* [Velveteen](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/112694) ♦ \- A little more fluff for tails (3) and (4). Comes in thick and thin sizes. Unisex.  
  * [Velveteen Tail ReTexture \- Miaou Tail](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/151689) ♦ \- Minor texture edit. Requires the original to work.

* [WITCH GARDEN](https://ko-fi.com/s/06331beee3) ♦ \- Changes tail (3) to make it extra thicc. Includes optional bow accessory. Female only.  
  * [Witch Garden Tail for Male Miqo](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/115992) ♦ \- Port to male Miqo’te. Replaces tail (3).

* [Zirconia Tails](https://mordproxy.gumroad.com/l/zirconia) ♦ \- Crystalline tail. 3 models \- Smooth, ‘Fluffy’, and Nine-Tailed. 2 options for transparent or opaque appearance. 4 color options. Includes optional starry sparkle. Replaces tail (3).

##### **9.2.6.3: Miqo’te Ear & Tail Combos**

	Mods that replace or remove both the ears and the tail at once. May or may not come with options to toggle one or the other off. Non-feline ears and tails can be found in the ‘Hybrid Resources’ section.

* [Abyssinian Fluff](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/114345) ♦ \- Combines ‘Abyssinian Ears’ and the ‘I Heard You Like Fluff’ tail. Replaces all tails. Female only.

* [Another Wolf Mashup](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/125150) ♦ \- Combines ‘PeePoo’ ears and Torgal’s tail model from ‘Good Boi and Girls’. Replaces tail (3). Unisex.

* [Arcadion Spoiler's Ears and Tail for Miqo'te](https://heliosphere.app/mod/phq3gczzvs01q92xht78j8b2p0) ♦ \- Ears and tail ripped from the Arcadion Boss 5’s model and given to Miqo’te. Multiple hair highlight options.

* [Bobcat Ears♡Tail](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/151503) ♦ \- Short and wide triangle-shaped ears with a short tail nub. Replaces tail (1). Female only.

* [Catte Fluff Mashup](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/120605) ♦ \- Combines ‘PeePoo’ ears and the ‘I heard you like Fluff’ tail. Replaces tail (3). Unisex.

* [Crystal Wolf](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/141389) ♦ \- Combines ‘Hypnotryst’ ears and ‘Zirconia’ tail. Replaces tail (3). Female only.  
  * [Crystal Wolf M](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/142911) ♦ \- Male port. Replaces tail (3).

* [Desert Warden](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/140230) ♦ \- Combines ‘Anpu Crown’ ears and ‘Sphynx’ tail. Dyes with hair and highlight color. Affects tail (1). Female only.

* [Female Miqo'te Vanilla Ear & Tail Remover](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/134937) ♦ \- Inner ear flap and tail removal in one mod. All faces, all tails.

* [FENNEC](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/92703) \- ‘Feline v1’ ears combined with ‘Puff Floff’ tail. Replaces all tails. Unisex.

* [FERAL](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/94348) \- Combines ‘Round-ish Ears’ and altered male Hrothgar tails. Replaces all tails. Unisex.

* [floofers (ears & tail)](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/111935) ♦ \- Massive, fluffy ears with a wide base. 2 tail sizes: Stubby and long. Replaces tail (8). Female only.

* [Forest Fluff Shagcoat](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/151636) ♦ \- ‘Forest Fluff’ ears and ‘Shagcoat Cat’ tail. Replaces tail (3). Female only.

* [Kitsune Ninetails](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/101509) \- ‘Peepoo Ears’ combined with ‘Super Fluff’ tail x9\! Highlight compatible. Replaces tail (3). Includes earless option to mix and match with other ears.

#### **9.2.7: Roegadyn Based**

* [Better Brows and Beard for Face 2 Roegadyns](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/135076) ♦ \- ‘Manderville’ beard and brow models ported to male Roegadyn face (2/102). 

* [~~Black Noses are for bears~~](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/2702) ~~\- Adjusts the female Roegadyn faces with an upscaled texture and removes the black nose tips.~~

* [Dark Brows for Fem Roe](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/65876) ♦ \- Gives female Roegadyn dark, undyeable brows that will not change to match hair color. Should work with sculpts.

* [Dog Noses for Hellsguard Males](https://www.nexusmods.com/finalfantasy14/mods/966) \- Adds a leathery texture to the black nose tip on all male Hellsguard faces (101, 102, 103, 104).

* [Double chin for F Roegadyn](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/141934) ♦ \- Reshapes the neck and jaw area to give all female Roegadyn faces a double chin. Vanilla (symmetrical) textures only. Retains all vanilla toggles and shapekeys. Includes optional edited ‘normal’ for a softer jaw.

* [Galka Tail for Male Roe](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/122706) ♦ \- A Galka tail for all your FFXI crossover needs. Technically also compatible with Hrothgar.

* [Handsome Roegadyn Lads](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/113847) ♦ \- Adjusts the textures for all male Roegadyn faces to be less wrinkly and generally younger.

* [Hellsgard Males- Brighter Eyes and Fixed Nose](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/87012) \- Whitens the sclera and removes the black tip from the noses of all Hellguard faces.

* [Hellsguard Roegadyn Skincare](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/14664) \- Smooths out the wrinkles on male Roegadyn faces (3) and (4). Works best in tandem with ‘Smooth Roes’.

* [Highlander Beard 104 on Male Roes](http://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/41601) ♦ \- Exactly what it says on the tin.

* [Hot Roes (Male Roe Face 4 104\)](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/19909) \- Thicker beard and eyebrows for male Roegadyn faces (4/104).  
  * [Hot Roes (Dawntrail)](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/117170#info) ♦ \- Unofficial Dawntrail update.

* [Roe Body Hair and Nipples Mod](https://www.nexusmods.com/finalfantasy14/mods/13) \- Adds a body hair texture and nipples to the vanilla male Roegadyn body. Includes 13 different hair patterns to choose from and one hairless/nipples only option.

* [Roegabeard: Classic Full](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/24678) \- Gives all male Roegadyn faces a thick, full beard.  
  * [Roegabeard: Classic Full](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/110209) ♦ \- DT upgrade for the original mod.

* [Roegabeard: Natural Full](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/58193) \- Gives all male Roe faces a long, full beard. Includes eyebrow option. Shows regardless of vanilla facial option. Not reaper eye compatible.  
  * [Roegabeard: Natural Full](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/110319) ♦ \- DT upgrade for the original mod.

* [Roegadyn Female EW face port](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/142160) ♦ \- Ye olde Endwalker face models directly ported to DT. Has neck seam. All faces.

* [Scruffy Beard for Fem Roe](https://heliosphere.app/mod/6c4qsvene92bv4fqmes0cpqt1w) ♦ \- A short fluffy beard for the ladies, on a ring. Fit to all vanilla faces.

* [~~Smallclothes Bulge for Roes~~](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/65) ~~\- Adds a large bulge to the vanilla body smallclothes.~~

* [Smoother Roes Face 4 Edit](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/118146) ♦ \- Smooths out the deeper wrinkles around the eyes and mouth for male Roegadyn face (4).

* [Smooth Roes](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/5096) \- Smooths out the male Roegadyn face texture to have less wrinkles.  
  * [Soft Roes](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/8267) \- Changes the male Roegadyn face textures to give a more soft, airbrushed look. Includes options to keep or remove the black nose tip.

#### **9.2.8: Viera Based**

* [Bunnified\! Male Viera Nose Facepaint](https://heliosphere.app/mod/fz1n0db90d0mf7738qdwgx9jkc) ♦ \- The signature Viera bunny nose on face paint, so you can layer it over your makeup mods. Made for all male Viera faces.

* [Bunny Plus](https://www.nexusmods.com/finalfantasy14/mods/836) ♦ \- A foot mod that changes your character’s feet to match the old Viera paws from FFXII. Compatible with TBSE and Bibo+. Includes brown and pink for pawpad color options, or no pawpads at all. Not compatible with ‘Lore Friendly Viera Feet’.  
  * [Bunny Peets](https://www.nexusmods.com/finalfantasy14/mods/678) \- Older version. Female only.

* [Dark Brows for Fem Viera](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/74059) ♦ \- Gives **female** Viera dark, undyeable brows that will not change to match hair color. Should work with sculpts.

* [Dark Brows for Viera Male](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/65756) ♦ \- Gives **male** Viera dark, undyeable brows that will not change to match hair color. Should work with sculpts.

* [DILF WORKSHOP \- Male Viera Wrinkles Resource](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/95535) ♦ \- Wrinkles texture for all male Viera faces. **Resource only** \- requires other programs to create the modpacks.

* [Double chin for F Viera](https://heliosphere.app/mod/gk982njxwn39q2t12v6hhz8vww) ♦ \- Changes the model and ‘normal’ map for all female Viera faces. Utilizes ‘Asym Faces’ as its base and includes ‘Default Bites’ teeth.

* [Earless F Viera](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/129130) ♦ \- Removes all female Viera ears by editing the textures instead of the model. Potentially compatible with custom sculpts. Does not cover ear holes in vanilla hairs.

* [Ephelis Face \- Viera \- Masc](https://trello.com/c/5FDlnphX/89-ephelis-face-viera-masc) \- Full face freckles and blush for all male Viera faces to match the TBSE Ephelis skin. Includes loose files, an optional multi, and a .PSD for full customization. Requires manual installation/conversion to work. **Highly recommended to use with the matching TBSE skin texture.**

* [Female Viera Earwiggles and Tailsway OVERHAULED](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/83052) ♦ \- Adds animations to female Viera ears and tails, if you use them. **\! Requires IVCS to work \!**

* [Freckles for F Viera (Vanilla and Asym faces)](https://www.glamourdresser.com/mods/f-viera-freckles) \- Dense freckles covering the upper cheeks and nose. Includes Vanilla (symmetrical) and AsymFaces options along with two transparent .PNG files for customizing.

* [Jackrabbit- Digitigrade Feet](https://www.glamourdresser.com/mods/jackrabbit-digitigrade-feet) \- A more ‘realistic’-looking approach to digitigrade feet for Viera. Technically works with all male Midlander-based bodies but currently only extensively tested on Viera. Replaces thighboots and behaves as such when worn with pants. Works best with the vanilla body but is compatible with TBSE.

* [Lore Friendly Viera Feet](https://www.nexusmods.com/finalfantasy14/mods/1692) \- A change to the foot model to make Viera feet resemble the type shown in FFXII and Tactics. Includes changes to an assortment of shoes and boots to accompany the new model. Not compatible with ‘Bunny Plus’ or ‘Bunny Peets’. ‘SimpleHeels’ use suggested to avoid floor clipping for everyday use.

* [M Viera Face 4 Adjustments](https://heliosphere.app/mod/saveysebzn1fs7kxaa9azxnkpg) ♦ \- Slight adjustments to the lips and textures to make the face closer to how it looked in Endwalker.

* [Male Viera Brighter Tongues](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/127735) ♦ \- Lightens the vanilla tongue color for all male Viera faces.

* [Male Viera Earwiggles and Tailsway](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/83048) ♦ \- Adds animations to male Viera ears and tails, if you use them. **\! Requires Skelomae to work \!**

* [Male Viera Face 3 \- Bunny Nose](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/110386) ♦ \- Lightens the bunny nose marking on male Viera face (3) to make it more visible. 

* [Male Viera Face 4 lips fix](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/118153) ♦ \- Changes the shape of the lips on male Viera face (4) to more closely resemble the shape pre-Dawntrail.

* [~~Male Viera Feet~~](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/2562) ~~\- A low-poly foot model matched to the vanilla model. Foot and ankles are not attached to the leg and they work like thigh-high boots and will cut-off longer pants.~~

* [Mimi's Tail \+ Masc Viera Ear 1 for Fem\!](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/107193) ♦ \- A combination of ‘Mimi’s Viera Tails’ and male Viera ear (1) for **female** Viera.

* [Nevus face \- Fem Viera](https://ko-fi.com/s/be02fbe381) \- Full face freckles with blush and skin damage for female Viera faces to match the Nevus skin mod. Includes loose files and a .PSD for customization. Uses AsymFaces and requires the base to work. Requires manual installation/conversion. **Highly recommended to use with the Bibo+ skin texture.**

* [Nose Smudge Restored (M Viera Face 1\)](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/110575) ♦ \- Restores the original darkened nose to male Viera face (1). Includes .PSD for customization for vanilla & Asym faces.

* [No Fuzz Viera Nose (For AsymFaces)](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/111349) ♦ \- Removes the marking on all female Viera noses. Uses AsymFaces as a base and requires it to work.

* [Scruffy Beard for Face 4/104 Viera](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/122136) ♦ \- A beard made from pieces of the male Miqo’te beard from faces (1) \+ (2). This is a model edit and therefore incompatible with other sculpts, but is compatible with makeup.

* [Scrungy (F4 M Viera)](https://heliosphere.app/mod/s5x4sxhw3x5cn6sdpf1gs7x9z4) ♦ \- Beard covering the jaw and chin, yoinked from male Miqo’te. Includes toggles for what to include/exclude. Affects face (4/104).

* [~~Simple Freckle Special Stand Alone~~](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/8936) ~~\- Symmetrical full-face freckles for female Viera face (2). Works best on mid-range skin tones.~~


* [~~Simply Freckles Galore~~](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/9001) ~~\- Symmetrical full-body freckles for female Viera on the vanilla body.~~

* [smudged nose for m viera face 3](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/126034) ♦ \- Puts the nose smudge onto male Viera face (3) as a toggle. This is a sculpt & texture edit that renders this mod incompatible with custom sculpts.

* [Stubble Viera Pack](https://heliosphere.app/mod/wc1eeqe98h0ssdd73enknamcxm) ♦ \- Beard stubble for male Viera. Affects all faces.

* [Subtle full body freckles for male viera](https://www.glamourdresser.com/mods/zc-%e2%98%86-subtle-full-body-freckles-for-male-viera) \- Symmetrical full-body freckles for male Viera. Made for vanilla bodies.

* [Sun Damage \[Male Viera F4\]](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/117694) ♦ \- Sunburn and freckles texture for male Viera face (4).

* [Sunkissed Freckles for Viera](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/160640) ♦ \- Clustered freckles across the cheeks and nose for all Viera faces. Unisex. Technically works for all races but made for Viera.

* [Viera Male 4 For Viera Female](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/79954) ♦ \- Male-to-Female face model swap. Uses Illybites teeth.

* [Viera Male Face 1 For Viera Females](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/70574) ♦ \- Male-to-Female face model swap. Uses Illybites teeth.

##### **9.2.8.1: Viera Ears**

* [Airplane Ears](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/125389) ♦ \- Moves the ear models (2) and (3) to point outward from the head horizontally. No bounce physics or weights. 8 highlight options. **Unisex**.

* [Colorful ears \- female viera](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/59181) / [male viera](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/57936) \- Changes the color of Viera ears to match hair highlights. Base ear fluff optional.

* [Cottontail Half Lop](https://heliosphere.app/mod/xy6tt9zcfx2qh9cv5hy61nbpdm) ♦ \- One lop ear and one upright ear. Affects female ear (1) and male ear (2). Includes ‘Cottontail Rabbit’ tail.

* [Death Bunny speckle ears for fem Viera](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/146534) ♦ \- Large spots for your bnuuy ears. Affects all ears. Female only.

* [ear one from female bun to male](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/84319) \- Ear model swap from female to male. Replaces ear (1).

* [Ears without highlighters\_V2](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/136700) ♦ \- Removes hair highlights from the ear tips. Affects all ears.

* [F\!Viera Ears for M\!Viera](https://heliosphere.app/mod/vyz2va5ymh5w1b3dgvhjtj2rm4) ♦ \- Female Viera ear types for males.

* [Female Viera Ear 1 to Male Viera Ear 1 \[DT\]](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/120306) ♦ \- Ear model swap for **male** Viera ear (1).

* [Floppa Ears](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/164241) ♦ \- Female Viera ear (1) turned downward. Includes optional tail from ‘Fluffy Tails’. Replaces **female** ear (1).

* [Floppiest Ears (for Male and Female Viera)](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/39325) \- Floppy ears that sit backwards on the head. Includes optional tail from ‘Floofy Viera Tails’. Replaces all ears.

* [Fran’s Ears](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/15302) \- Changes **female** Viera ear (4) to Fran’s ear model.

* [FtM Viera Ears](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/122845) ♦ \- Ear model swap for all **male** Viera ears to the female versions.

* [Half Lop](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/39112)  \- Changes Viera ears to have one be lop while the other is upright. Uses Viera ear models (3) and (4). Two-tone color option available.


* [Half-Lop for Male Viera & Optional Tail](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/39105) \- Changes **male** Viera ears (2) and (4), making one side lop-eared.

* [Lop ear (Floppy ear)](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/147237) ♦ \- One lop ear and one upright ear. Includes bouncy physics. Replaces ear (2). Male only.

* [XTRA FLUFF \- Viera M ear 1](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/98405) \- Tall ears with inner ear fluff. Replaces ear (1). Male only.

### **9.3: 🐾 Peets**

	“Peets” is a widely supported mod that changes the shape of your character’s feet to a small-pawed digitigrade foot. Since this changes the model of the foot to be taller, the use of the plug-in “Simple Heels” is highly recommended if you intend on regular use.

For more information on the Peets mods and the gear made for them, please refer to [this master spreadsheet](https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/1h0y7KCZEuC8WF9kWTn8e2R4l86hdrNTlvQJXfDnh5JQ/). A devkit for textures, all models and more can be found [here](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1jQKPXb-QvjzCkniTnVhwtEgE7YzOOb3u?usp=sharing). (Link shared with permission, thank you SeaBastardd\!)

	Base mods (do not need another Peets mod to work):

* [Peets](https://www.deviantart.com/bizuart/art/Peets-905069826) (original, female only)  
* [~~Lalafell Peets~~](https://beta.aetherlink.app/mods/6978295182970355712) ~~(Port to the Lalafell body)~~  
* [~~Male Peets~~](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/44316) ~~(outdated)~~  
* [~~Male Peets 2.0~~](https://www.glamourdresser.com/mods/male-peets-2-0) ~~(male only)~~  
* [Peets 3.0 ReFurred](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/90170) ♦ \- Updated to work with all current Peets models with remodeled claws. Standalone \- does not require any older Peets mods to work. Compatible with most Bibo+ and TBSE-based bodies, and Feral Paws. Includes Male Roegadyn support. 3 claw variants available.  
  * [ReFurred Gen3 Add-On](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/124961) ♦  
  * [Peets ReFurred \- Fabulous Trans Men Compatibility](https://heliosphere.app/mod/7kng6vkfc94ezcfwvgzswgehz0) ♦

* [Hrothgal Peets](https://heliosphere.app/mod/2v9rp1mded3df1468gnexhyarr) ♦ \- All Peets models (Standard, Smol, Puppy, Wabbit) for female Hrothgar in one place.

* [Toe Tips Add-On](https://heliosphere.app/mod/qpz63w97ex5hsbx3g8stkctgxr) ♦ \- Colored toes for your peets. 4 color combinations. Made for ReFurred and Smol Peets. Not Hrothgar Peets compatible.

* [Wabbit Peets](https://heliosphere.app/mod/m4r1hdtff95a999wp0881665kr) ♦ \- A new Peets shape (2025) with a more narrow but longer foot. Compatible with other add-ons such as ReFurred, Claws and Beans textures. Model-only; Requires a base Peets mod to work.

## **Part 10: Hybrid Resources**

Is your character a mix of two races and you want to showcase that with a different body part or look? Do you like the faces or ears of one race, but your character is another? Making a character that isn’t a playable race at all, like a fish person or a wolf? What about a chimaera or an amalgamation of whatever-the-fuck? This section is for you\!

#### **10.01: Au Ra Based**

* [~~A Biblically Correct Tail for M Au Ra~~](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/136130) ~~♦ \- An oddly fuzzy tail with wings ripped from an endgame Endwalker boss. Includes toggle to turn wings off. One color. Replaces all tails. Male only.~~  
  * [a biblically correct tail for femra](https://heliosphere.app/mod/qx0zbsy1r91psdnrczjepdzbqg) ♦ \- Female port. Replaces all tails.

* [Almost scaleless](https://heliosphere.app/mod/aq6s7t6at132k8g3yf48ze3ksw) ♦ \- Removes the majority of the body scales from male Au Ra while keeping the scales at the neck. Includes black/white color options.

* [Alternative Flop: F AuRa](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/159297) ♦ \- ‘Alternative Flop’ Viera ears (plus tail, optionally) but for Au Ra. Replaces all tails. Female only.

* [~~Arbiter's Tail~~](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/149255) ~~♦ \- Furred tail with feathered bits on the side, yoinked from a dungeon boss. Replaces tail (3/103). Male only.~~

* [Au Ra Elf Ears 2.0 \+ Horns and Options](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/19718) ♦ \- Comes with the option to replace Au Ra horns with elf ears, or replace the horns with ears and move the horns higher on the head. Works with scaleless faces on both male and female models.

* [Blue Tongues for Xaela (2 opt)](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/117541) ♦ \- Texture edit to make the tongues blue for Xaela. 2 color options. Unisex.

* [Bone Tail](https://ko-fi.com/s/ea478872d0) \- Replaces tail (1/101). Female only.

* [Bun’ra Bunny Ears for Au Ra](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/77204) \- Female Viera ear (1) to all Au Ra tails.

* [Creep Tail](https://ko-fi.com/s/cc766c3a47) ♦ \- Extra long, extra thin, and splits into two towards the tip. 2 colors. Unisex.

* [Croa \- Ears & Horns (F AuRa)](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/87601) \- Elf ears and horns that curl up over the head. Female only.

* [~~Darya the Sea-maid \- Male Au Ra tail 3~~](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/157307) ~~♦ \- Tail model taken from a Variant Dungeon boss. Replaces tail (3/103).~~  
  * [Darya Sea Maid Port to Aura Fem Tail 4](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/157574) ♦ \- Female Au Ra port. Replaces tail (4).

* [Decorative Heat Tail](https://ko-fi.com/s/a21b5e4498) \- Thin, scaleless tail with a heart-shaped tip. Includes decor. 3 color variants. Female only.

* [Deer for aura f](https://heliosphere.app/mod/addzvw1qh97yfb9w6588ddmr9m) ♦ \- Port of ‘Deer’ ears, horns, and tail to female Au Ra.

* [dragon (ears & tail)](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/111447) ♦ \- Big horns on top of the head paired with long pierced elf ears that replace vanilla horns. Tail is large, long, and furry. Replaces tail (2/102). Female only.

* [Elf-Ra](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/70898) \- Removes the horns from all Au Ra faces and replaces them with small elf ears. Scaleless only.

* [Elf'ra](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/110420) ♦ \- Removes horns from all Au Ra faces and replaces them with elf ears. Works on Raen and Xaela. Unisex. Uses ‘Aysm Faces’ as a base. Not compatible with custom sculpts. YMMV with earrings.

* [~~Elfra – Elf Ears for Female Aura~~](https://www.glamourdresser.com/mods/elfra-elf-ears-for-female-aura) ~~\- Replaces female Au Ra horns with elf ears. Includes 5 blush color options for the ear tips. Equipped with The Emperor’s New Hat.~~

* [F Au'ra Scaleless Neck Seam Fix](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/105638) \- Fixes the neck seam for female scaleless Au Ra. 

* [Floppy Eared Aura](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/133121) ♦ \- Takes male Viera ear (2) and puts it onto male Au Ra face (1/101).

* [Fluuffy miqo tail for miqo'ra](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/20210) \- Replaces all tails with Miqo’te tail (3). Female only.

* [Flying Sparks for masc au ra](https://heliosphere.app/mod/jpzfxetbgn0cd97npv91192tvc) ♦ \- Scales recolor and tail inspired by Monster Hunter. Replaces all tails. Male only.

* [HD Scaleless](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/15767) \- Removes scales from the female Au Ra bodies and faces and removes the horns. Uses ‘Female Refined’ as a base skin.

* [Hornless Aura F1/F2](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/50105) \- Removes the horns from female Au Ra face (1/101) and (2/102). Does not cover ear holes.

* [Hornless Aura F3/4](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/50305) \- Removes the horns from female Au Ra face (3/103) and (4/104). Does not cover ear holes.

* [Horns begone (hornless man ra faces)](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/115150) ♦ \- Removes horns from all vanilla male Au Ra faces.

* [Hyur ears for Au'ra\!](https://www.patreon.com/posts/hyur-ears-for-57887933) \- Replaces vanilla horns with Hyur ears on all male faces.

* [I wanna be a Carbuncle (Tail)](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/93733) ♦ | [Ears](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/93738) ♦ \- Carbuncle tail and ears in separate mod packs. Replaces tails (1, 2, 3). Ears do not hide horns. Both available in 6 colors.

* [Kamuy Of The Nine Tails Set for Au Ra](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/17699) \- Reshapes the Tamamo headband ears and takes the tails from the Kamuy mount. Replaces all tails. Female only.

#### **10.09: Multiple Races**

* [A Biblically Correct Tail on a Ring](https://heliosphere.app/mod/hvx93vp0812cn189x7pp59a9hg) ♦ \- ‘A Biblically Correct Tail’ on an accessory. Compatible with all races and genders except female Lalafell and male Hrothgar. Does not auto-hide tails from tailed races.

* [Alpaconse](https://heliosphere.app/mod/ea5kjyk3116h19711j4bhbje28) ♦ \- Horse-like ears and tail made with vanilla assets. Replaces tail and ears for Miqo’te, Au Ra, Viera, and Hrothgar. Equippable option for all other races. 4 color options for HrAMV, 2 for everyone else. Non-tailed races require any skeleton mod (IVCS2, YAS, Skelomae) to work.

  * [Sharps \- IVCS addon](https://heliosphere.app/mod/9atrk77mp509na10vtb6syxbtg) ♦ \- Hand-length model only. Midlander-based models only. Unisex.

* [Sin'dorei](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/116433) ♦ \- Long Blood Elf ears from WoW. 2 models, 3 sizes to choose from. Unisex. All races sans Hrothgar.

## **Part 11: Texture Upscales**

For the texture upscales. There are plenty of options to choose from, and they’re completely optional \- so pick and choose these at your leisure. However, if your computer has a low graphics card or out-of-date system and is prone to crashing I personally recommend avoiding using upscales. These should also be avoided if you intend on using Mare Synchronos.

* [1k vanilla facepaints](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/26304) \- Upscales the vanilla face paints to look less pixelated. Face paint 25 (eyebrows) has been redrawn from scratch, and face paint 26 (freckles) is not included.

* [2K Face Diffuse Resource \[DT\]](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/109780) ♦ \- Increases the resolution of the vanilla Dawntrail faces. 2 options available: A base 2k size upscale with no edits, and a smoother texture upscaled with AI and edited. Includes Clanless Miqo’te and clean noses for specific races.

* [~~2K Facelift~~](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/1269) ~~\- Texture upscale for all player race faces.~~


* [~~2K Hairlift~~](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/1268) ~~\- Texture upscale for all player character hairs (as of 2020).~~

* [4K skin for Bibo+/Gen3](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/64655) ♦ \- 4K skin texture pack including tan lines, scars, body freckles, and Au Ra scales.

* [~~AstralEcho’s Adonis Pack~~](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/212) ~~\- 2K skin textures with new normal and specular maps, including Au Ra tails and Au Ra pubic scales. Male Highlander, Midlander, Au Ra, Elezen, and Miqo’te only.~~


* [AsymFaces \- Compendium & Resources](https://www.xivmodarchive.com/modid/31453) \- A masterlist of every AsymFaces texture to work with if you want to make your own mods, or want a makeup that uses AsymFaces as its base. Each head is an altered sculpt and is not compatible with other model edits. Includes vanilla and custom teeth options.

## **F.A.Q**

Please direct any and all questions and concerns to my [Twitter](https://twitter.com/MooncatKeeper) or my [BlueSky](https://bsky.app/profile/mooncatkeeper.bsky.social). If I get asked the same question enough times I’ll put it here for everyone to refer to. Just remember to keep your messages kind and courteous or they will be ignored. If your message does not get a response within a week it may have been tucked away under message requests or been otherwise blocked or hidden from my viewing (thanks Twitter).

\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_\_

* “You don’t have X Body/I can’t find X body\!”

If it’s a base body and not a texture add-on, it is most likely already here and will be under “Base Bodies” or their respective tabs. Please click on them to open the sections and use “ctrl \+ F” to find exactly what you’re looking for. If it turns out that I do not have the mod though, thank you for bringing it to me\! It will be noted to be added at my earliest convenience.

* “What does low/medium/high support mean?”

The ‘support’ is made in reference to how many *publicly available* gear mods are made for the body, combined with how popular it is in the community to the best of my knowledge. A body with the ‘low support’ tag means that there are likely only a few outfits for the body and that the body itself isn’t very popular. ‘No support’ means that while there may be gear mods made for it, the body has either been discontinued or abandoned. A body edit intended for nude use only will not have a support tag.

* “What are the qualifications for a mod to be put on the compendium? Can my mod be on here?”

If your mod is free and publicly available, then the only other qualifications required to be here are that it must affect one or more of the following: Any piece of the main body model (chest, legs, hands, or feet), skin texture (diffuse, normal, or multi), ears, tails, teeth, Au Ra horns, and some face textures. I will not accept or list tattoos, makeup, wings, or standard face sculpts. I will also NOT accept any mods that are found to be made with or alongside Generative AI usage.
